								<a class="d-block text-decoration-none" href="https://magazine.fany.lol/tag/osaka-comedy-festival-2026/">
									<div class="featured-img px-0 position-relative">
																						<figure class="mt-0 mb-2 post-thumbnail">
			<img width="800" height="600"   alt="出典: FANY マガジン" decoding="async" data-srcset="https://magazine.fany.lol/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/7f7953617ec31c0105cf41a61820c35b.png 800w, https://magazine.fany.lol/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/7f7953617ec31c0105cf41a61820c35b-350x263.png 350w"  data-src="https://magazine.fany.lol/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/7f7953617ec31c0105cf41a61820c35b.png" data-sizes="(max-width: 800px) 100vw, 800px" class="attachment-メイン size-メイン lazyload" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" />		</figure>
											</div>
									<div class="fw-bold mb-0 theme-t-09 theme-lg-t-10">

	<section class="py-4 py-lg-5 theme-bg-light-pink theme-front-writers">
		<div class="container">
			<h2 class="theme-t-15 theme-lg-t-20 lh-sm mb-3">芸人ライター・芸人カメラマン</h2>
			<div class="slick pb-3 pb-lg-4 mx-4">
